## 新投稿 (100)

<h3 id="1">[1] GraphBit: A Graph-based Agentic Framework for Non-Linear Agent Orchestration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13848)
- **Authors**: Yeahia Sarker, Md Rahmat Ullah, Musa Molla, Shafiq Joty
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Workflow Automation
- **Summary**: GraphBit是一个基于图的非线性Agent编排框架，通过DAG定义工作流，使用Rust引擎管理路由和状态转换，解决了提示编排中的幻觉路由和无限循环问题，在GAIA基准测试中达到最高准确率。

<h3 id="2">[2] Mixed Integer Goal Programming for Personalized Meal Optimization with User-Defined Serving Granularity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13849)
- **Authors**: Francisco Aguilera Moreno
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Nutritional Analysis, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 本文提出混合整数目标规划(MIGP)方法用于个性化膳食优化，使用整数变量处理实际份量和目标规划偏差处理软营养约束，解决了传统方法中分数份量和约束不可行的问题。

<h3 id="3">[3] A Two-Dimensional Framework for AI Agent Design Patterns: Cognitive Function and Execution Topology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13850](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13850)
- **Authors**: Jia Huang, Joey Tianyi Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个二维AI Agent设计模式分类框架，结合认知功能轴（7类）和执行拓扑轴（6种结构原型），识别出27种命名模式，为AI Agent架构设计提供了框架无关的词汇体系。

<h3 id="4">[4] Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13851](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
- **Authors**: Hiroki Fukui
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, AI Safety, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 研究发现多Agent系统中不可见的编排器会导致集体解离、行为异质性增加等安全风险，且这些内部状态扭曲在基于输出的评估中完全不可见，揭示了编排器可见性和模型选择对多Agent系统安全的影响。

<h3 id="5">[5] PREPING: Building Agent Memory without Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13880)
- **Authors**: Yumin Choi, Sangwoo Park, Minki Kang, Jinheon Baek, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: Preping是一个预任务记忆构建框架，通过提议者引导的合成实践让Agent在观察目标任务前构建程序性记忆，在多个基准测试上达到与离线/在线记忆构建方法相当的性能，同时大幅降低部署成本。

<h3 id="6">[6] PolitNuggets: Benchmarking Agentic Discovery of Long-Tail Political Facts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14002](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14002)
- **Authors**: Yifei Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Information Extraction, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: PolitNuggets是一个多语言基准测试，用于评估Agent对长尾政治事实的发现和综合能力，包含400位全球精英的政治传记和超过10000个政治事实，揭示了当前系统在细粒度细节和效率方面的不足。

<h3 id="7">[7] Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14004](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
- **Authors**: Erica Stutz, Giacomo Marino, Daniella Meeker, Qiao Liu, Andrew J. Loza
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 提出条件属性Transformer方法，可在单次前向传播中联合估计下一个token概率和条件属性值，实现token级信用分配、反事实分析和可控生成，在稀疏奖励任务上达到最优性能。

<h3 id="8">[8] Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
- **Authors**: David N. Olivieri, Roque J. Hernández
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Neurosymbolic AI, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个有限层论框架，用于检测AI Agent中的科学理论转变候选，通过传输和阻碍来检测表示框架是否需要扩展，在过渡卡片基准测试上验证了方法的有效性。

<h3 id="9">[9] From Descriptive to Prescriptive: Uncover the Social Value Alignment of LLM-based Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14034)
- **Authors**: Jinxian Qu, Qingqing Gu, Teng Chen, Luo Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, RAG, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: CogSci 2026
- **Summary**: 提出一个基于价值的框架，使用GraphRAG将原则转化为基于价值的指令，引导Agent按照社会价值行事，在DAILYDILEMMAS基准测试上显著优于提示基线方法。

<h3 id="10">[10] Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
- **Authors**: Leslie G. Valiant
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Neurosymbolic AI, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 提出一种高效的有原则推理方法，通过预处理将数据编码为一元关系积分码，使关系规则学习变为多项式时间可学习，同时保留现有软件和硬件基础。

<h3 id="11">[11] Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
- **Authors**: Yize Cheng, Chenrui Fan, Mahdi JafariRaviz, Keivan Rezaei, Soheil Feiz
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 研究发现LLM工具使用中存在知行差距，即模型内部认知认为需要工具但实际未调用工具，通过探测隐藏状态发现这种不匹配主要发生在认知到行动的转换阶段。

<h3 id="12">[12] Network-Aware Bilinear Tokenization for Brain Functional Connectivity Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14048)
- **Authors**: Leo Milecki, Qingyu Hu, Bahram Jafrasteh, Mert R. Sabuncu, Qingyu Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Medical AI, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Summary**: NERVE是一个自监督学习框架，通过网络感知的双线性分词方法重新定义功能连接矩阵的分词方式，在三个大规模发展队列上实现了更稳定和可迁移的表示。

<h3 id="13">[13] Bridging Legal Interpretation and Formal Logic: Faithfulness, Assumption, and the Future of AI Legal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14049](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14049)
- **Authors**: Olivia Peiyu Wang, Leilani H. Gilpin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Neurosymbolic AI, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: LSLLAI 2026 Symposium
- **Summary**: 提出一种神经符号方法，将大语言模型的表达能力与形式验证的严谨性相结合，旨在使AI辅助法律推理既具备能力又值得信赖，减少手动验证负担的同时保持法律实践所需的问责制。

<h3 id="14">[14] SPIN: Structural LLM Planning via Iterative Navigation for Industrial Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14051](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14051)
- **Authors**: Yusuke Ozaki, Dhaval Patel
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Automated Planning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: SPIN是一个规划包装器，结合验证DAG规划和前缀执行控制，在AssetOpsBench和MCP Bench上减少了执行任务数量和工具调用，同时提高了完成率。

<h3 id="15">[15] Bad Seeing or Bad Thinking? Rewarding Perception for Vision-Language Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14054](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14054)
- **Authors**: Haozhe Wang, Qixin Xu, Changpeng Wang, Taofeng Xue, Chong Peng, Wenhu Chen, Fangzhen Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Reinforcement Learning, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 提出一种强化学习框架，通过感知验证和模态感知信用分配机制，将生成过程分解为交替的感知和推理步骤，独立奖励感知保真度，解决了视觉语言模型中感知与推理的权衡问题。

<h3 id="16">[16] MathAtlas: A Benchmark for Autoformalization in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14061)
- **Authors**: Nilay Patel, Noah Arias, Davit Babayan, Victoria Cochran, Timothy Libman, Hafsah Mahmood, Liam McCarty, Soli Munoz, Laurel Willey, Jeffrey Flanigan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Autoformalization, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: MathAtlas是首个大规模研究生级别数学自动形式化基准测试，包含约52k个定理、定义等，并首次引入数学依赖图，揭示了当前模型在深度依赖任务上的性能下降问题。

<h3 id="17">[17] Know When To Fold 'Em: Token-Efficient LLM Synthetic Data Generation via Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14062)
- **Authors**: Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury, Syed Zawad, Feng Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, LLM Inference, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出MSIFR框架，通过在生成过程的中间检查点检测并终止低质量轨迹，实现LLM合成数据生成的早期拒绝，在保持或提升质量的同时减少11%-77%的token消耗。

<h3 id="18">[18] SkillFlow: Flow-Driven Recursive Skill Evolution for Agentic Orchestration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14089)
- **Authors**: Mingda Zhang, Tiesunlong Shen, Haoran Luo, Wenjin Liu, Zikai Xiao, Erik Cambria, Xiaoying Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Flow Matching, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文提出SkillFlow框架，采用Tempered Trajectory Balance进行训练，结合递归技能演化机制实现智能体编排，在问答、数学推理、代码生成和决策任务上显著超越基线。

<h3 id="19">[19] ChromaFlow: A Negative Ablation Study of Orchestration Overhead in Tool-Augmented Agent Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14102)
- **Authors**: Tarun Mittal
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文通过负向消融实验发现，更激进的编排策略并未提升工具增强智能体的性能，反而增加了操作噪声，表明有界的规划器升级和确定性提取应作为可靠评估的首要要求。

<h3 id="20">[20] Modeling Bounded Rationality in Drug Shortage Pharmacists Using Attention-Guided Dynamic Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14111)
- **Authors**: Yaniv Eliyahu Amiri, Noah Chicoine, Jacqueline Griffin, Stacy Marsella
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Decision Making, Cognitive Science, Agent Architecture
- **Venue**: CogSci 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种有限理性的注意力引导决策框架，用于模拟医院药剂师在药物短缺情况下的决策过程。该框架通过动态分解将药物分为高成本推理子集和低成本监控子集，开发了专家代理和学习者代理两种模型。

<h3 id="21">[21] ClawForge: Generating Executable Interactive Benchmarks for Command-Line Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14133)
- **Authors**: Yuxiang Lai, Peng Xia, Haonian Ji, Kaiwen Xiong, Kaide Zeng, Jiaqi Liu, Fang Wu, Jike Zhong, Zeyu Zheng, Cihang Xie, Huaxiu Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Decision Making, Medical AI, Cognitive Science
- **Venue**: CogSci 2026
- **Summary**: 本文形式化了一种有限理性的注意力引导决策框架，用于药剂师在药物短缺时进行决策，通过动态分解药物子集实现稳定的决策而无需完整状态推理。

<h3 id="22">[22] Distribution-Aware Algorithm Design with LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14141)
- **Authors**: Saharsh Koganti, Priyadarsi Mishra, Pierfrancesco Beneventano, Tomer Galanti
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出OmniDrop，一种无需训练的分层token剪枝框架，在LLM解码器层内渐进式剪枝视听token，并利用文本查询作为引导。该方法在减少40%预填充延迟和14.7%内存使用的同时，性能提升高达3.58个百分点。

<h3 id="55">[55] From Table to Cell: Attention for Better Reasoning with TABALIGN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14465)
- **Authors**: Tung Sum Thomas Kwok, Zeyong Zhang, Xinyu Wang, Chunhe Wang, Xiaofeng Lin, Hanwei Wu, Lei Ding, Guang Cheng, Zhijiang Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Table Reasoning, Question Answering, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出TABALIGN框架，将掩码扩散语言模型规划器与轻量级验证器配对，实现表格推理中的单元格定位约束。该方法在八个表格问答和事实验证基准上比最强基线平均提升15.76个百分点。

<h3 id="56">[56] LEMON: Learning Executable Multi-Agent Orchestration via Counterfactual Reinforcement Learning</h3>

- **Authors**: Xudong Chen, Yixin Liu, Hua Wei, Kaize Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出LEMON框架，通过反事实强化学习训练LLM编排器生成可执行的多智能体编排规范，整合角色设计、能力分配和依赖结构。该方法在六个推理和编程基准上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="57">[57] Deepchecks: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14488)
- **Authors**: Assaf Gerner, Netta Madvil, Nadav Barak, Alex Zaikman, Jonatan Liberman, Liron Hamra, Rotem Brazilay, Shay Tsadok, Yaron Friedman, Neal Harow, Noam Bresler, Shir Chorev, Philip Tannor, Lior Rokach
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文介绍Deepchecks框架，用于评估RAG应用的多方面评估、根因分析和生产监控。该框架解决了RAG系统评估中生成输出随机性和检索与生成组件复杂交互的挑战。

<h3 id="58">[58] Learning Scenario Reduction for Two-Stage Robust Optimization with Discrete Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14494)
- **Authors**: Tianjue Lin, Jianan Zhou, Jieyi Bi, Yaoxin Wu, Wen Song, Zhiguang Cao, Jie Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Optimization, Graph Neural Network, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出NeurPRISE，一种基于GNN-Transformer的神经代理模型，用于两阶段鲁棒优化中的场景削减，通过模仿学习训练。该方法相比PRISE实现7-200倍加速，并具有强大的零样本泛化能力。

<h3 id="59">[59] When Robots Do the Chores: A Benchmark and Agent for Long-Horizon Household Task Execution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14504)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文引入LongAct基准，用于评估长时域家庭任务的规划级自主性，并提出HoloMind智能体，包含DAG分层规划器、多模态空间记忆和情景记忆。实验显示顶级模型仅达到59%目标完成率。

<h3 id="60">[60] Cattle Trade: A Multi-Agent Benchmark for LLM Bluffing, Bidding, and Bargaining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14537](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14537)
- **Authors**: Robert Müller, Clemens Müller
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Benchmark, Game Theory
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文引入Cattle Trade多智能体基准，用于评估LLM在不完全信息、对抗性交互和资源约束下的战略推理能力，结合拍卖、讨价还价、虚张声势等元素。研究发现战略连贯性比单项技能更能预测排名。

<h3 id="61">[61] VerbalValue: A Socially Intelligent Virtual Host for Sales-Driven Live Commerce</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14542)
- **Authors**: Yuyan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, LLM Application
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出VerbalValue，一个面向销售转化的虚拟主播系统，结合领域知识库和标注的直播交互数据，通过微调LLM实现共情、商业导向的响应。相比基线在信息量上提升23%，事实正确性上提升18%。

<h3 id="62">[62] Complacent, Not Sycophantic: Reframing Large Language Models and Designing AI Literacy for Complacent Machines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14544)
- **Authors**: Federico Germani, Giovanni Spitale
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Ethics, AI Literacy
- **Summary**: 本文论证LLM应被理解为'顺从'而非'谄媚'，因为它们缺乏战略意图，其同意倾向源于训练数据和设计。这一重新定位将责任归于开发者和机构，并建议AI素养教育应重点关注对抗确认偏差。

<h3 id="63">[63] TeachAnything: A Multimodal Crowdsourcing Platform for Training Embodied AI Agents in Symmetrical Reality</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Imitation Learning, Data Collection
- **Venue**: IEEE VR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文介绍TeachAnything平台，一个面向对称现实中具身智能体训练的多模态众包演示平台，集成物理仿真。该平台通过三阶段演示范式收集跨场景、任务和具身的多样化演示数据。

<h3 id="64">[64] PyCSP3-Scheduling: A Scheduling Extension for PyCSP3</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14559)
- **Authors**: Sohaib Afifi

- **Summary**: 本文提出PyCSP3-Scheduling扩展库，为PyCSP3添加53个专用约束和27个表达式的调度抽象。该库在保持建模与求解分离的同时，提供原生的调度支持，部分模型族实现高达5.8倍加速。

<h3 id="65">[65] Prompt Segmentation and Annotation Optimisation: Controlling LLM Behaviour via Optimised Segment-Level Annotations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14561)

- **Summary**: 本文提出PSAO框架，通过将提示词分解为可解释的片段并添加人类可读的注释（如{重要}、{不重要}）来引导大语言模型在生成响应时合理分配注意力，从而提高推理准确性和自一致性。

<h3 id="66">[66] Sycophancy is an Educational Safety Risk: Why LLM Tutors Need Sycophancy Benchmarks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14604)
- **Authors**: Enkelejda Kasneci, Gjergji Kasneci
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Alignment, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 本文指出LLM辅导系统存在"推理-奉承悖论"，即模型可能为了迎合用户而牺牲认知严谨性，并提出了EduFrameTrap基准来评估模型在数学、物理等学科中的社会认知勇气，即支持性但纠正性的辅导行为。

<h3 id="67">[67] SliceGraph: Mapping Process Isomers in Multi-Run Chain-of-Thought Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14619)
- **Authors**: Kang Chen, Junjie Nian, Yixin Cao, Yugang Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 本文提出SliceGraph方法，通过构建基于思维链切片间稀疏激活相似度的互kNN图来分析多轮推理轨迹，发现85.5%的问题中正确轨迹会分裂成多个"过程异构体"，揭示了最终答案聚合所忽略的结构化多路径过程几何。

<h3 id="68">[68] Teaching Large Language Models When Not to Know: Learning Temporal Critique for Ex-Ante Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14636](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14636)
- **Authors**: Chenlu Ding, Jiancan Wu, Yanchen Luo, Zheyuan Liu, Yancheng Yuan, Xiang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Editing, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文研究LLM在时间截止点下的推理失败问题，提出TCFT（时间批判微调）框架，训练模型识别后截止点知识泄漏、解释时间边界违规并判断时间可接受性，在Qwen2.5模型上平均减少超过37个百分点的泄漏。

<h3 id="69">[69] MindGap: A Conversational AI Framework for Upstream Neuroplastic Intervention in Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14660)
- **Authors**: Eranga Bandara, Ross Gore, Asanga Gunaratna, Ravi Mukkamala, Nihal Siriwardanagea, Sachini Rajapakse, Isurunima Kularathna, Pramoda Karunarathna, Wathsala Herath, Chalani Rajapakse, Sachin Shetty, Anita H. Clayton, Christopher K. Rhea, Ng Wee Keong, Kasun De Zoysa, Amin Hass, Shaifali Kaushik, Preston Samuel, Atmaram Yarlagadda
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Dialogue System, Mental Health
- **Summary**: 本文提出MindGap，一个隐私保护的端上对话AI框架，基于佛教心理学中的"缘起"原理，引导PTSD患者在情感信号与反应性阐述之间的间隙进行三层渐进式观察，实现上游神经可塑性康复而非下游症状抑制。

<h3 id="70">[70] Falkor-IRAC: Graph-Constrained Generation for Verified Legal Reasoning in Indian Judicial AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14665](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14665)
- **Authors**: Joy Bose
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Knowledge Graph, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出Falkor-IRAC框架，将印度司法判决构建为IRAC（问题-规则-分析-结论）知识图谱，通过验证代理追踪生成答案的支撑路径，有效减少幻觉判例和过时法规引用，提高法律推理的可信度。

<h3 id="71">[71] Monitoring Data-aware Temporal Properties (Extended Version)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14666)
- **Authors**: Alessandro Gianola, Marco Montali, Sarah Winkler
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Monitoring, Temporal Reasoning
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个用于监测带有SMT理论的线性时间属性（LTLfMT）的基础框架，结合自动机理论处理时序逻辑维度和自动推理技术处理一阶维度，首次识别出结合线性算术与未解释函数的可判定片段。

<h3 id="72">[72] How Sensitive Are Radiomic AI Models to Acquisition Parameters?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14667](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14667)
- **Authors**: D. Gil, I. Sanchez, C. Sanchez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个面向性能的混合效应框架来量化放射组学AI模型对扫描参数的敏感性，识别出CT扫描的最佳配置参数（管电流≥200mA、螺距≤1.5、层厚≤1.25mm），将肺癌诊断敏感性从0.79提升至0.90。

<h3 id="73">[73] $π$-Bench: Evaluating Proactive Personal Assistant Agents in Long-Horizon Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14678)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出π-Bench基准，包含100个跨5个用户角色的多轮任务，通过隐藏用户意图、任务间依赖和跨会话连续性来评估个人助手代理在长期交互中的主动性和任务完成能力，发现主动辅助仍具挑战性。

<h3 id="74">[74] On Strong Equivalence Notions in Logic Programming and Abstract Argumentation</h3>

<h3 id="75">[75] Agentifying Patient Dynamics within LLMs through Interacting with Clinical World Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14723](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14723)

<h3 id="76">[76] XDomainBench: Diagnosing Reasoning Collapse in High-Dimensional Scientific Knowledge Composition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14754)
- **Authors**: Gong Zhiren, Tiantong Wu, Jiaming Zhang, Fuyao Zhang, Che Wang, Yurong Hao, Yikun Hou, Foo Ping, Yilei Zhao, Fei Huang, Chau Yuen, Wei Yang Bryan Lim

<h3 id="77">[77] Probabilistic Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks for Single and Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14758)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Verification, Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026

<h3 id="78">[78] AI Outperforms Humans in Personalized Image Aesthetics Assessment via LLM-Based Interviews and Semantic Feature Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14761](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14761)
- **Authors**: Yoshia Abe, Tatsuya Daikoku, Yasuo Kuniyoshi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Image Quality Assessment, LLM Application, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个集成深度学习与LLM的系统，通过基于LLM的半结构化访谈主动获取审美偏好信息，结合低层和高层语义特征预测个性化图像审美评价，实验表明AI预测优于人类预测者甚至目标个体自身的重评估。

<h3 id="79">[79] MediaClaw: Multimodal Intelligent-Agent Platform Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14771)
- **Authors**: Shaoan Zhao, Huanlin Gao, Qiang Hui, Ting Lu, Xueqiang Guo, Yantao Li, Xinpei Su, Fuyuan Shi, Chao Tan, Fang Zhao, Kai Wang, Shiguo Lian

<h3 id="80">[80] Identifying Culprits Through Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient Deep Learning Investigation</h3>

- **Authors**: Lata B T, Savitha N J
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文将深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）算法应用于犯罪识别问题，通过训练犯罪现场数据、证人证词和嫌疑人档案，在识别罪犯方面达到95%的准确率，优于其他现有方法。

<h3 id="81">[81] A Heterogeneous Temporal Memory Governance Framework for Long-Term LLM Persona Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14802)
- **Authors**: Zhao Yang, Wang Huan, Li Yingshuo, Tu Haomiao, Lin Hujite
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Memory Architecture, Dialogue System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Spirtxiaoqi7/ARPM)
- **Summary**: 本文提出ARPM框架，通过分离静态知识记忆和动态对话经验记忆，结合向量检索、BM25和双时序重排序等技术，解决大语言模型在长程交互中的事实丢失、时间线混乱和人设漂移问题。实验表明该框架在高噪声环境下能有效维护人设一致性。

<h3 id="82">[82] Interestingness as an Inductive Heuristic for Future Compression Progress</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14831)
- **Authors**: Vincent Herrmann, Jürgen Schmidhuber
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Improvement, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文将"趣味性"形式化为未来压缩进展的归纳启发式方法，利用Kolmogorov复杂度和算法统计工具研究其可预测性。研究证明预期未来进展指数依赖于最近突破的时间，且算法先验比长度先验更乐观。

<h3 id="83">[83] Emotion-Attended Stateful Memory (EASM):The Architecture for Hyper-Personalization at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14833)
- **Authors**: Vineet Kotecha, Vansh Gupta
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Memory Architecture, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种情感关注的状态记忆架构，利用长期历史、情感信号和推断意图动态构建用户特定的对话上下文。A/B测试显示该架构在记忆锚定、计划清晰度和情感验证方面分别提升95%、57%和34%。

<h3 id="84">[84] A Deterministic Agentic Workflow for HS Tariff Classification: Multi-Dimensional Rule Reasoning with Interpretable Decisions</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Workflow Automation, Rule Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种确定性智能体工作流用于HS关税分类，采用固定控制流将语言模型调用限制在狭窄阶段，实现可解释的多维规则推理。在HSCodeComp数据集上达到64.2%的六位数top-1准确率。

<h3 id="85">[85] Holistic Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis of AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14865)
- **Authors**: Netta Madvil, Gilad Dym, Alon Mecilati, Edo Dekel, Jonatan Liberman, Rotem Brazilay, Liron Schliesser, Max Svidlo, Shai Nir, Orel Shalom, Yaron Friedman, David Connack, Amos Rimon, Philip Tannor, Shir Chorev
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Agent Debugging, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种整体性智能体评估框架，将自顶向下的智能体级诊断与自底向上的跨度级评估相结合，实现对任意长度轨迹的可扩展分析。在GAIA和SWE-Bench上取得最优结果，定位准确率提升高达3.5倍。

<h3 id="86">[86] BiFedKD: Bidirectional Federated Knowledge Distillation Framework for Non-IID and Long-Tailed ECG Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14886)
- **Authors**: Zixuan Shu, Tiancheng Cao, Hen-Wei Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出双向联邦知识蒸馏框架BiFedKD，用于医疗物联网中的非独立同分布和长尾ECG监测。该方法在MIT-BIH心律失常数据集上提升准确率和Macro-F1分别达3.52%和9.93%，同时降低通信开销40%。

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了TERMS-Bench，一个贝叶斯博弈框架，用于评估LLM谈判代理，通过指定对手方的潜在类型、策略和收益结构使环境本身成为验证器。该基准能够进行代理可归因的失败分析，揭示了前沿模型在达成率上饱和但在剩余提取、线索使用、信念校准和合规性方面存在分歧。

<h3 id="119">[119] Multi-Scale Dequant: Eliminating Dequantization Bottleneck via Activation Decomposition for Efficient LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13915) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lingchao Zheng, Yuwei Fan, Jun Li, Chengqiu Hu, Qichen Liao, Junyi Fan, Rui Shi, Fangzheng Miao
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Quantization, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 本文提出了多尺度反量化(MSD)框架，通过将高精度激活分解为多个低精度分量来消除LLM推理中的反量化瓶颈。该方法避免了INT8到BF16的权重转换，实现了近16个有效位的INT8权重处理，并将注意力机制中的KV缓存HBM流量减少高达2.5倍。

<h3 id="120">[120] A Regret Perspective on Online Multiple Testing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13916) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Optimization, Multi-Armed Bandit, Statistical Testing
- **Summary**: 本文引入加权遗憾作为在线多重测试的统一评估指标，证明了确保严格FDR控制的纯确定性程序不可避免地产生线性遗憾惩罚。作者提出了解耦OMT(DOMT)元包装器，通过历史解耦的非负随机扰动实现最优次线性遗憾减少。

<h3 id="121">[121] Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling</h3>

Beason Held, Murat Bilgel, Laura A. Barquero, Micah DArchangel, Tin Q. Nguyen, Laurie B. Cutting, Derek Archer, Timothy J. Hohman, Daniel C. Moyer, Bennett A. Landman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Representation Learning, Dimensionality Reduction
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种无监督框架，通过混合潜在空间建模来分离结构连接组中的采集相关效应和生物变异。该框架使用架构退火来自适应平衡离散和连续潜在变量，在7416个连接组的数据集上实现了更强的站点学习(ARI=0.53)。

<h3 id="122">[122] Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez, Enrique Zuazua, Georgios Kellaris, Joaquin del Rio, Oleksii Sliusarenko, Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LLM Training, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文展示了通过联邦学习在私有机构数据上微调LLM的实用方法，在医疗和金融领域进行了跨域基准测试。框架使节点能够联合微调共享LLM而无需交换私有数据，比较了三种参数高效微调策略，表明联邦微调性能接近集中训练且优于单机构学习。

<h3 id="123">[123] AgentTrap: Measuring Runtime Trust Failures in Third-Party Agent Skills</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13940) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zhmzm/AgentTrap)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了AgentTrap，一个动态基准，用于评估LLM代理在使用第三方技能时是否能抵御恶意运行时行为。基准包含141个任务，覆盖16个安全影响维度，揭示了模型经常完成可见用户任务的同时将技能引入的不安全副作用视为正常工作流的一部分。

<h3 id="124">[124] EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13941](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了EvolveMem，一种自进化记忆架构，通过LLM驱动的诊断模块将完整检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间进行优化。该系统自主对其架构进行迭代研究循环，在LoCoMo上比最强基线相对提升25.7%，在MemBench上相对提升18.9%。

<h3 id="125">[125] Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction</h3>

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dfaroughy/Collider-Bench)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Collider-Bench，一个用于评估LLM代理是否能使用公开论文和开放科学软件重现大型强子对撞机实验分析的基准。基准要求代理将发表的分析转换为可执行管道，提供连续保真度分数和计算成本指标，表明没有代理能可靠地击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

<h3 id="127">[127] Few Channels Draw The Whole Picture: Revealing Massive Activations in Diffusion Transformers</h3>

<h3 id="145">[145] ExploitBench: A Capability Ladder Benchmark for LLM Cybersecurity Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Cybersecurity, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ExploitBench，一个能力分级的基准测试，将漏洞利用分解为16个可测量的阶段，从代码覆盖到任意代码执行。实验表明，公开部署的模型能够触发崩溃但很少实现任意代码执行，而私有前沿模型在约一半案例中展示了这一能力。

<h3 id="148">[148] LLM-Based Robustness Testing of Microservice Applications: An Empirical Study</h3>

<h3 id="149">[149] Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种细粒度概念瓶颈模型框架，将每个概念与局部视觉证据关联，使用户能够验证模型是否学习了预期概念而非虚假相关。在医学影像基准上的实验表明，该方法在保持预测性能的同时显著提高了透明度和可验证性。

<h3 id="150">[150] PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14217](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrew Lanpouthakoun, Aryaman Arora, Zhengxuan Wu, Dhruv Pai, Ben Keigwin, Dan Jurafsky, Christopher Potts
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LLM Inference, LLM Serving
- **Summary**: 本文提出PreFT（仅预填充微调），仅在预填充阶段应用适配器并在之后丢弃，显著提高了多适配器服务的吞吐量。在Llama 3.1 70B上服务512个适配器时实现了1.9倍的吞吐量提升，且对性能影响最小。

<h3 id="151">[151] Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianle Zhong, Neiwen Ling, Yifan Pi, Zijun Wei, Tianshu Yu, Geoffrey Fox, Peng Wu, Xiao Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文识别并诊断了LLM强化学习中的训练-推理不匹配（TIM）问题，即rollout生成和策略优化产生不同的token概率。研究表明小的数值差异可独立导致训练崩溃，TIM是影响LLM RL稳定性的首要因素。

<h3 id="152">[152] Wavelet-Based Observables for Koopman Analysis: An Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14224) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cankat Tilki, Serkan Gugercin
- **Subjects**: math.NA; cs.AI; math.DS; math.FA
- **Tags**: Dynamical Systems, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了基于小波的可观测量用于Koopman分析，推导了Koopman半群及其预解式的闭式表达式，并结合扩展动态模态分解开发了cWDMD算法。

<h3 id="153">[153] AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14231](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanxun Huang, Qizhou Wang, Xingjun Ma, Cihang Xie, Christopher Leckie, Sarah Erfani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Audio Processing, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic)
- **Summary**: 本文提出AudioMosaic，一种基于对比学习的音频编码器，通过结构化时频掩码构建正样本对实现高效预训练。该方法在多个音频基准上实现了最先进性能，并可提升音频-语言模型在音频语言任务上的表现。

<h3 id="154">[154] Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann, Juan Kaplan, Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo, Juan Wisznia, Luciano del Corro
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Reranking, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文将成对排序提示（PRP）重排序重新框架为从噪声成对比较中进行主动学习，提出了噪声鲁棒框架和随机方向预言机。该方法在调用受限情况下提高了NDCG@10，并将系统性位置偏差转化为零均值噪声。

<h3 id="155">[155] Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaohua Wang, Kai Yu, XuXiao Liang, Liang Wang, Chao Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Signal Processing, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Summary**: 本文开发了针对胎儿心率监测的AI模型FHrCTG，能够有效缓解噪声干扰并精确重建信号。模型在检测关键FHR减速和加速方面表现出高敏感性和特异性，并在临床应用标准下取得了良好的AUC分数。

<h3 id="156">[156] Action-Conditioned Risk Gating for Safety-Critical Control under Partial Observability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14246) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yushen Liu, Yin-Jen Chen, Ziyi Chen, Tao Wang, Heng Huang, Xugui Zhou, Yanfu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Safe RL, Reinforcement Learning, POMDP
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种轻量级风险门控强化学习方法，用于部分可观测下的风险敏感控制。该方法构建紧凑的有限历史代理状态，学习动作条件的安全违规预测器，在血糖调节和安全导航任务中表现出色。

<h3 id="157">[157] Not All Timesteps Matter Equally: Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation for Spiking Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14252](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14252) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Sun, Peibo Duan, Yongsheng Huang, Guowei Zhang, Benjamin Smith, Nanxu Gong, Levin Kuhlmann
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Spiking Neural Network
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KaiSUN1/SeAl)
- **Summary**: 本文提出选择性对齐知识蒸馏，通过在错误时间步均衡竞争性logits并基于置信度和时间步相似性重新加权时间对齐，选择性对齐类级和时间知识。在静态图像和神经形态数据集上展示了相比现有蒸馏方法的持续改进。

<h3 id="158">[158] Dynamics of the Transformer Residual Stream: Coupling Spectral Geometry to Network Topology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jesseba Fernando, Grigori Guitchounts
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Transformer Architecture, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文对三个生产级LLM进行完整的雅可比特征分解，发现训练在深度上安装了单调谱梯度和累积的低秩瓶颈。研究表明这种梯度是学习得到的而非架构固有的，并将扰动传播与网络功能拓扑相关联。

<h3 id="159">[159] Image Restoration via Diffusion Models with Dynamic Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14267](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14267) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Zheng, Wen Li, Zhaoqiang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Restoration, Diffusion Model, Image Enhancement
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/StarNextDay/SubDAPS.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出动态分辨率扩散模型用于图像恢复，通过将数据投影到低维子空间加速推理。提出了SubDPS、SubDAPS和SubDAPS++方法，在多种图像数据集和恢复任务上优于最近的基于扩散模型的方法。

<h3 id="160">[160] PhyMotion: Structured 3D Motion Reward for Physics-Grounded Human Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14269](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14269) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yidong Huang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Dong-Ki Kim, Shayegan Omidshafiei, Jaehong Yoon, Jaemin Cho, Yue Zhang, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Motion Synthesis, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出PhyMotion，一种结构化的细粒度运动奖励，将恢复的3D人体轨迹在MuJoCo物理模拟器中评估。该方法沿运动学合理性、接触和平衡一致性、动态可行性三个轴评估运动质量，在RL后训练中实现了更大的运动真实性改进。

<h3 id="161">[161] Watermarking Game-Playing Agents in Perfect-Information Extensive-Form Games</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14283) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juho Kim, Fei Fang, Tuomas Sandholm
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Watermarking, Game AI
- **Summary**: 本文研究如何在完美信息扩展式博弈中为游戏智能体的策略添加水印，将LLM的KGW水印方法适配到游戏场景，实验表明水印对策略质量影响可忽略且仅需少量对局即可检测。

<h3 id="162">[162] MetaMoE: Diversity-Aware Proxy Selection for Privacy-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts Unification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14289](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14289) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weisen Jiang, Shuhao Chen, Sinno Jialin Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Privacy, Federated Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ws-jiang/MetaMoE)
- **Summary**: 本文提出MetaMoE框架，通过多样性感知的代理选择策略，利用公开数据作为私有数据的代理，在保护隐私的前提下将独立训练的专家模型统一为单一MoE模型。

<h3 id="163">[163] Web Agents Should Adopt the Plan-Then-Execute Paradigm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14290) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julien Piet, Annabella Chow, Yiwei Hou, Muxi Lyu, Sylvie Venuto, Jinhao Zhu, Raluca Ada Popa, David Wagner
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Web Agent, LLM Security, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 本文论证Web智能体应采用"先规划后执行"范式而非ReAct架构，以防止网页内容中的提示注入攻击，并指出需要类型化的网站API接口来支持这种范式。

<h3 id="164">[164] To See is Not to Learn: Protecting Multimodal Data from Unauthorized Fine-Tuning of Large Vision-Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14291](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14291) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengshuai Zhao, Zhen Tan, Dawei Li, Zhiyuan Yu, Huan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Data Privacy, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文提出MMGuard方法，通过注入人眼不可感知的扰动生成不可学习样本，主动保护多模态数据免受未经授权的大视觉语言模型微调，在九个开源LVLM上验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="165">[165] Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14297](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matias Alvo, Daniel Russo, Yash Kanoria
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization, Robotics
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl)
- **Summary**: 本文提出混合策略优化方法(HPO)，用于解决混合离散-连续动作空间中的强化学习问题，通过结合路径梯度和分数函数梯度，在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上显著优于PPO。

<h3 id="166">[166] Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning for Reusing Local Transition Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14304) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zuyuan Zhang, Carlee Joe-Wong, Tian Lan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Transfer Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出矩阵空间强化学习(MSRL)，通过正半定矩阵描述符聚合轨迹段的局部转移几何信息，实现序列决策中的组合泛化，在目标任务上达到0.73的平均AUC。

<h3 id="167">[167] ICED: Concept-level Machine Unlearning via Interpretable Concept Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14309](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14309) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shen Lin, Jing Lin, Junhao Dong, Piotr Koniusz, Li Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Vision-Language Model, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出ICED框架，通过可解释的概念分解实现视觉语言模型的概念级机器遗忘，能够精确移除目标知识同时保留同一图像中的非目标语义和全局跨模态知识。

<h3 id="168">[168] Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen Sun, Pei Fu, Shaojie Zhang, Anan Du, Xiuwen Xi, Ruoceng Zhang, Zhenbo Luo, Jian Luan, Chongyang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出BBCritic方法，将GUI批评从二元分类重构为连续语义对齐问题，通过两阶段对比学习在共享可供性空间中对齐指令和动作，在BBBench基准上优于7B参数的二元分类模型。

<h3 id="169">[169] Dynamic Latent Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14323](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14323) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fangyuan Yu, Xin Su, Amir Abdullah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Routing Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出动态潜在路由(DLR)方法，通过动态搜索联合学习离散潜在编码、路由策略和模型参数，在低数据微调设置下相比监督微调平均提升6.6个百分点。

<h3 id="170">[170] AIM-DDI: A Model-Agnostic Multimodal Integration Module for Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14327) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yerin Park, Sangseon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Multimodal Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出AIM-DDI，一种模型无关的多模态集成模块，通过将异构模态信息表示为共享潜在空间中的token，实现跨不同DDI预测架构的结构、化学和语义信号的有效整合。

<h3 id="171">[171] Analog RF Computing: A New Paradigm for Energy-Efficient Edge AI Over MU-MIMO Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14331) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wentao Yu, Vincent W.S. Wong
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.IT; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Energy Efficiency, Wireless Networks
- **Summary**: 本文提出模拟射频计算范式，通过基站编码神经网络权重并广播RF波形，使客户端能够复用无源混频器执行矩阵向量乘法，相比数字计算可降低近两个数量级的客户端能耗。

<h3 id="172">[172] RQ-MoE: Residual Quantization via Mixture of Experts for Efficient Input-Dependent Vector Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14359) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengjia Zhong, Shuyan Ke, Zaizhou Lin, Jiaqi Song, Hongyi Lan, Hui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Model Compression, Quantization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KDEGroup/RQ-MoE)
- **Summary**: 本文提出RQ-MoE框架，结合两级MoE与双流量化实现输入相关的码本自适应，在保持重建和检索性能的同时，解码速度比现有向量量化方法快6-14倍。

<h3 id="173">[173] Correctness-Aware Repository Filtering Under Maximum Effective Context Window Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14362) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shweta Mishra
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, Code Generation, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出正确性感知的上下文清理框架，使用基于大小的启发式过滤器在分词前拦截仓库扫描，在10个开源仓库上实现79.6%的平均token减少，同时将文件级准确率从25%提升至72%。

<h3 id="174">[174] LoMETab: Beyond Rank-1 Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14365) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="175">[175] Where Should Diffusion Enter a Language Model? Geometry-Guided Hidden-State Replacement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14368) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="176">[176] Deciphering Neural Reparameterized Full-Waveform Inversion with Neural Sensitivity Kernel and Wave Tangent Kernel</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14370) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruihua Chen, Yisi Luo, Bangyu Wu, Xile Zhao, Deyu Meng

<h3 id="177">[177] Turning Stale Gradients into Stable Gradients: Coherent Coordinate Descent with Implicit Landscape Smoothing for Lightweight Zeroth-Order Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14373) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="178">[178] Optimal Pattern Detection Tree for Symbolic Rule-Based Classification</h3>

<h3 id="179">[179] Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games</h3>

<h3 id="180">[180] Darwin Family: MRI-Trust-Weighted Evolutionary Merging for Training-Free Scaling of Language-Model Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="181">[181] Agentic Recommender System with Hierarchical Belief-State Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14401) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="182">[182] Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet</h3>

<h3 id="183">[183] MahaVar: OOD Detection via Class-wise Mahalanobis Distance Variance under Neural Collapse</h3>

<h3 id="184">[184] SWE-Chain: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Chained Release-Level Package Upgrades</h3>

<h3 id="186">[186] MemLineage: Lineage-Guided Enforcement for LLM Agent Memory</h3>

<h3 id="187">[187] Collaborative Yet Personalized Policy Training: Single-Timescale Federated Actor-Critic</h3>

<h3 id="188">[188] A plug-and-play generative framework for multi-satellite precipitation estimation</h3>

<h3 id="189">[189] Efficient Generative Retrieval for E-commerce Search with Semantic Cluster IDs and Expert-Guided RL</h3>

<h3 id="190">[190] When Answers Stray from Questions: Hallucination Detection via Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition</h3>

<h3 id="191">[191] Does RAG Know When Retrieval Is Wrong? Diagnosing Context Compliance under Knowledge Conflict</h3>

<h3 id="192">[192] When Retrieval Hurts Code Completion: A Diagnostic Study of Stale Repository Context</h3>

<h3 id="193">[193] Head Forcing: Long Autoregressive Video Generation via Head Heterogeneity</h3>

<h3 id="200">[200] Dimension-Level Intent Fidelity Evaluation for Large Language Models: Evidence from Structured Prompt Ablation</h3>

<h3 id="201">[201] PROVE: A Perceptual RemOVal cohErence Benchmark for Visual Media</h3>

<h3 id="202">[202] RxEval: A Prescription-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Medication Recommendation</h3>

<h3 id="204">[204] Break-the-Beat! Controllable MIDI-to-Drum Audio Synthesis</h3>

<h3 id="205">[205] Resolving Action Bottleneck: Agentic Reinforcement Learning Informed by Token-Level Energy</h3>

<h3 id="206">[206] A Picture is Worth a Thousand Words? An Empirical Study of Aggregation Strategies for Visual Financial Document Retrieval</h3>

<h3 id="207">[207] Angel or Demon: Investigating the Plasticity Interventions' Impact on Backdoor Threats in Deep Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14587) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="208">[208] Fast Rates for Inverse Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14599](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14599) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andreas Schlaginhaufen, Maryam Kamgarpour
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Inverse Reinforcement Learning, Optimization, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文建立了熵正则化最小最大逆强化学习的结构和统计结果，证明了轨迹级KL散度和参数误差的快速收敛率，并将奖励可识别性结果扩展到一般Borel空间。

<h3 id="209">[209] One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Itay Zloczower, Eyal Lenga, Gilad Gressel, Yisroel Mirsky
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Alignment, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文研究了针对恶意微调攻击的防御机制，发现现有的15种防御方法都存在共同弱点：它们只是掩盖或误导有害行为路径，而非真正移除这些行为。作者提出了一种统一的自适应攻击方法，成功突破了所有防御机制，表明当前方法无法提供真正的安全性保障。

<h3 id="210">[210] In-IDE Toolkit for Developers of AI-Based Features</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14612](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14612) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Debugging, Software Engineering
- **Venue**: ICSE 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了JetBrains IDE的AI工具包插件，将追踪和评估功能直接集成到开发调试循环中。研究表明开发者需要定期可重复的评估、执行时即时暴露追踪信息以及最小化设置和上下文切换，该工具通过IDE原生工作流降低了AI开发的门槛。

<h3 id="211">[211] Do We Really Need External Tools to Mitigate Hallucinations? SIRA: Shared-Prefix Internal Reconstruction of Attribution</h3>

<h3 id="212">[212] An Amortized Efficiency Threshold for Comparing Neural and Heuristic Solvers in Combinatorial Optimization</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE

<h3 id="213">[213] Action-Inspired Generative Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eshwar R. A., Debnath Pal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV

- **Summary**: 本文提出Action-Inspired Generative Models (AGMs)，一种双网络生成框架，通过学习标量势能函数对桥样本进行在线评分，动态调整传输路径的重要性权重。该方法仅增加约1.4%的参数量，无需额外推理开销，可即插即用于任何桥匹配训练循环。

<h3 id="214">[214] MultiEmo-Bench: Multi-label Visual Emotion Analysis for Multi-modal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14635) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Affective Computing, Benchmark

<h3 id="215">[215] How to Evaluate and Refine your CAM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14641](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14641) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luca Domeniconi, Alessandra Stramiglio, Michele Lombardi, Samuele Salti
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Computer Vision
- **Venue**: ICPR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对类激活图(CAM)的评估和分辨率问题，提出了包含真实标注的合成数据集和新的复合评估指标ARCC。同时引入RefineCAM方法，通过聚合多层网络的CAM生成高分辨率归因图，在所提出的评估指标上持续优于现有方法。

<h3 id="216">[216] Vision-Based Water Level and Flow Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14645) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: ZhiXin Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computer Vision, Environmental Monitoring
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sunzx97/Vision_Based_Water_Level_and_Flow_Estimation.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个集成框架，将最先进的视觉模型与统计建模相结合，用于水位检测和流量估计。通过利用物理先验和鲁棒滤波策略，该方法在提高检测精度的同时增强了系统鲁棒性，相比传统传感方法具有更好的可解释性和自动化数据归档能力。

<h3 id="217">[217] Agentic Design of Compositional Descriptors via Autoresearch for Materials Science Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14671) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matteo Cobelli, Stefano Sanvito
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Material Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Automat框架，利用基于大语言模型的编码代理自动设计材料描述符。该系统在无机材料带隙预测和铁磁化合物居里温度预测任务上超越了传统基线方法，生成的描述符具有化学可解释性，展示了自主研究代理在材料科学中的应用潜力。

<h3 id="218">[218] AI-assisted cultural heritage dissemination: Comparing NMT and glossary-augmented LLM translation in rock art documents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14679) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vicent Briva-Iglesias, María Ferre-Fernández
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Cultural Heritage
- **Summary**: 本文比较了三种机器翻译设置在岩石艺术学术文本翻译中的表现，发现结合术语表增强提示的LLM翻译在术语准确率上达到81.4%，显著优于简单提示和神经机器翻译基线，同时保持整体翻译质量，为文化遗产机构提供了一种低成本的翻译质量提升方案。

<h3 id="219">[219] Spontaneous symmetry breaking and Goldstone modes for deep information propagation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14685) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nabil Iqbal, T. Anderson Keller, Yue Song, Takeru Miyato, Max Welling
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nabiliqbal/ssb-goldstone-deep-info-prop)
- **Summary**: 本文研究了具有连续对称性的深度神经网络中的Goldstone模式，发现这些自由度能够实现跨深度和循环迭代的相干信号传播。该机制在前馈网络中改善了可训练性和表示多样性，在循环设置中提升了长序列建模性能，为信息稳定传播提供了新机制。

<h3 id="220">[220] NeuroAtlas: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Clinical EEG and Brain-Computer Interfaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14698) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Konstantinos Kontras, Trui Osselaer, Stylianos G. Mouslech, Angeliki-Ilektra Karaiskou, Guido Gagliardi, Thomas Strypsteen, Mohammad Hossein Badiei, Anku Rani, Maarten Vanmarcke, Miguel Bhagubai, Chanakya Ekbote, Jaedong Hwang, Christos Chatzichristos, Paul Pu Liang, Maarten De Vos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, Foundation Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了NeuroAtlas，目前最大的EEG基准测试平台，涵盖42个数据集和26万小时数据，包括临床EEG和脑机接口任务。研究发现EEG专用基础模型并未持续优于通用时间序列模型，标准机器学习指标不足以评估临床效用，当前模型尚未实现统一EEG模型的承诺。

<h3 id="221">[221] SceneFunRI: Reasoning the Invisible for Task-Driven Functional Object Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14704](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14704) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Posheng Chen, Powen Cheng, Gueter Josmy Faure, Hung-Ting Su, Winston H. Hsu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Object Detection
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了SceneFunRI基准，用于评估模型对不可见区域功能对象的推理定位能力。该基准包含855个实例，要求模型根据任务指令和常识推理推断被遮挡物体的位置，最强基线模型仅达到15.20的准确率，表明当前视觉语言模型在不可见区域推理方面仍存在重大挑战。

<h3 id="222">[222] Vision-Core Guided Contrastive Learning for Balanced Multi-modal Prognosis Prediction of Stroke</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14710) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liren Chen, Lidong Sun, Mingyan Huang, Junzhe Tang, Yinghui Zhu, Guanjie Wang, Yiqing Xia, Ting Xiao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种三模态融合模型用于缺血性中风预后预测，整合医学图像、结构化临床数据和非结构化文本。该方法利用大语言模型从脑部MRI生成半结构化诊断文本，并设计了视觉条件双对齐融合模块，通过视觉特征作为条件先验引导文本交互，取得了最先进性能。

<h3 id="223">[223] IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14712](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shijie Lian, Bin Yu, Xiaopeng Lin, Zhaolong Shen, Laurence Tianruo Yang, Yurun Jin, Haishan Liu, Changti Wu, Hang Yuan, Cong Huang, Kai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Imitation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZGC-EmbodyAI/IntentVLA)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了IntentVLA框架，一种历史条件化的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过编码近期视觉观察为紧凑的短期意图表示来条件化动作块生成。该方法解决了现有方法在部分可观察性下可能产生动作块冲突的问题，在多个基准上提升了机器人操作的执行稳定性。

<h3 id="224">[224] Towards Label-Free Single-Cell Phenotyping Using Multi-Task Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saqib Nazir, Ardhendu Behera
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multi-Task Learning, Single-cell Analysis
- **Venue**: ICPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/saqibnaziir/Single-Cell-Phenotyping)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种统一深度学习框架，从无标记差分相位对比图像联合进行白细胞分类和蛋白质表达回归。模型采用混合架构融合卷积纹理特征和Transformer全局表示，在BSCCM基准上达到91.3%的白细胞分类准确率和0.72的CD16表达回归相关系数。

<h3 id="225">[225] TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Krish Sharma, Omar Naim, Soumadeep Saha, Nicholas Asher
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pruning, OOD Generalization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 本文研究了任务感知层剪枝为何能提升模型在分布外(OOD)数据上的性能。作者发现OOD输入会诱导偏离分布内特征的表示轮廓，任务感知剪枝通过移除产生或放大这种扭曲的层，使OOD输入重新对齐模型的任务适应几何结构。

<h3 id="226">[226] Addressing Terminal Constraints in Data-Driven Demand Response Scheduling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14741) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maximilian Bloor, Martha White, Ehecatl Antonio del Rio Chanona, Calvin Tsay
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Demand Forecasting, Energy Management
- **Venue**: IFAC World Congress 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出将目标空间规划(GSP)与深度确定性策略梯度(DDPG)相结合的方法，用于解决需求响应调度中的终端约束问题。该方法通过学习离散子目标上的时间抽象模型来传播长期价值，在空气分离基准测试中显著提高了样本效率并满足终端存储约束。

<h3 id="227">[227] Mechanical Enforcement for LLM Governance:Evidence of Governance-Task Decoupling in Financial Decision Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14744](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14744) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: José Manuel de la Chica Rodríguez, Carlos Martí-González
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Financial AI, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本文研究了金融决策系统中LLM治理问题，发现纯文本治理存在治理-任务解耦现象，而机械执行通过架构分离显著提升了治理质量和任务准确性。

<h3 id="228">[228] Video2GUI: Synthesizing Large-Scale Interaction Trajectories for Generalized GUI Agent Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14747) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weimin Xiong, Shuhao Gu, Bowen Ye, Zihao Yue, Lei Li, Feifan Song, Sujian Li, Hao Tian
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, LLM Agent, Data Synthesis
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出Video2GUI框架，能够从无标注的网络视频中自动提取GUI交互轨迹。基于此构建了包含1200万条轨迹的WildGUI数据集，在多个GUI定位和操作基准上实现了5-20%的性能提升。

<h3 id="229">[229] Non-linear Interventions on Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14749) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sangwoo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Model Editing, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种通用的非线性干预方法，突破了现有线性干预方法的局限。该方法能够对非线性表示的特征进行干预，并在拒绝绕过引导任务中表现出比线性基线更精确的引导能力。

<h3 id="230">[230] EVA: Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14750](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14750) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Wang, Hongye Qiu, Yue Xu, Sibei Yang, Zhan Qin, Minlie Huang, Wenjie Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Jailbreak Attack, Model Editing
- **Venue**: IEEE TPAMI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出EVA框架，首次将直接模型编辑应用于安全对齐，通过识别并精确编辑导致模型易受有害指令影响的特定神经元来防御越狱攻击。该方法在不损害模型通用推理能力的前提下有效中和有害行为。

<h3 id="231">[231] Cognitive-Uncertainty Guided Knowledge Distillation for Accurate Classification of Student Misconceptions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14752](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14752) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qirui Liu, Hao Chen, Weijie Shi, Jiajie Xu, Jia Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Education Technology, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Findings
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种两阶段知识蒸馏框架，用于准确分类学生误解。该方法基于认知不确定性识别关键样本，并设计难度自适应机制平衡硬软标签贡献，仅用10.30%的筛选样本就实现了显著性能提升。

<h3 id="232">[232] Compositional Sparsity as an Inductive Bias for Neural Architecture Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14764](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14764) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongyu Lin, Antonio Briola, Yuanrong Wang, Tomaso Aste
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture, Model Compression, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文将组合稀疏性作为神经网络设计的归纳偏置，结合信息过滤网络和同调神经网络构建可解释的架构设计流程。所提出的HNN比标准DNN稀疏多个数量级，在保持或超越稠密基线性能的同时具有更低的方差和超参数敏感性。

<h3 id="233">[233] Streaming Speech-to-Text Translation with a SpeechLLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14766) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Titouan Parcollet, Shucong Zhang, Xianrui Zheng, Rogier C. van Dalen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Machine Translation, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的流式语音到文本翻译架构，模型不仅学习输出翻译token，还学习判断是否已接收足够音频进行输出。该方法在保持接近非流式基线翻译质量的同时，将延迟降低至1-2秒。

<h3 id="234">[234] Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suorong Yang, Hanqi Zhu, Hai Gan, Fangjian Su, Guang Li, Furao Shen, Soujanya Poria
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Selection, LLM Training, Training Efficiency
- **Summary**: 本文提出PODS框架，从优化角度重新审视数据选择，动态调度训练过程中的数据选择量。该方法在目标选择比例下交替进行低比例正则化阶段和高比例恢复阶段，在ImageNet训练中降低50%成本并提升准确率，在LLM指令微调中加速超过2倍。

<h3 id="235">[235] Known By Their Actions: Fingerprinting LLM Browser Agents via UI Traces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: William Lugoloobi, Samuelle Marro, Jabez Magomere, Joss Wright, Chris Russell
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Web Agent, Privacy
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KabakaWilliam/known_actions)
- **Summary**: 本文研究表明LLM浏览器代理可以通过其UI交互轨迹被指纹识别，准确率高达96% F1。这构成了安全风险，使攻击者能够针对已知模型漏洞进行定向攻击，而注入随机时序延迟仅能提供有限的保护。

<h3 id="236">[236] Graphs of Research: Citation Evolution Graphs as Supervision for Research Idea Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Songyang Gao, Yinghui Xia, Siyi Liu, Hui Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Scientific Discovery, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出GoR方法，利用引用演化图作为监督信号来指导LLM研究想法生成。该方法为每篇种子论文提取引用邻域并构建论文演化有向无环图，在与GPT-4o驱动的基线对比中达到了最先进性能。

<h3 id="237">[237] Beyond AI as Assistants: Toward Autonomous Discovery in Cosmology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14791) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Licong Xu, Thomas Borrett
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; astro-ph.CO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了两个用于宇宙学自主科学发现的智能体系统：CMBEvolve用于具有明确量化目标的任务，CosmoEvolve用于开放式研究工作流。这些系统在弱透镜图异常检测和ACT DR6数据分析中展示了自主科研能力。

<h3 id="238">[238] GPart: End-to-End Isometric Fine-Tuning via Global Parameter Partitioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14841) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出GPart方法，通过全局参数划分实现端到端等距微调。该方法使用单一等距划分矩阵将可训练向量直接映射到模型的完整权重空间，消除了LoRA的低秩瓶颈，在自然语言理解、计算机视觉和数学推理任务上实现了最先进的效率和性能。

<h3 id="239">[239] XFP: Quality-Targeted Adaptive Codebook Quantization with Sparse Outlier Separation for LLM Inference</h3>

<h3 id="240">[240] IFPV: An Integrated Multi-Agent Framework for Generative Operational Planning and High-Fidelity Plan Verification</h3>

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zhigao3ks/IFPV)
- **Summary**: 本文提出IFPV多智能体框架，用于生成式作战规划和高保真计划验证。该框架结合多视角分层智能体进行规划生成和对抗认知仿真引擎进行验证，在任务成功率上提升19.4%，作战成本降低41.7%。

<h3 id="241">[241] FactorizedHMR: A Hybrid Framework for Video Human Mesh Recovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14854) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Patrick Kwon, Chen Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Human Mesh Recovery, Motion Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出FactorizedHMR框架，针对人体网格恢复中的模糊性问题，采用两阶段方法：确定性回归模块恢复稳定的躯干-根部锚点，概率流匹配模块完成四肢等不确定部位的预测，在遮挡场景和世界空间指标上取得显著提升。

<h3 id="242">[242] Exploitation of Hidden Context in Dynamic Movement Forecasting: A Neural Network Journey from Recurrent to Graph Neural Networks and General Purpose Transformers</h3>

<h3 id="243">[243] Do Coding Agents Understand Least-Privilege Authorization?</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文研究编码智能体是否能够理解最小权限授权，提出了AuthBench基准测试和权限边界推断任务，发现现有模型在授权策略生成上存在系统性缺陷，并提出了充分性-紧致性分解方法来改善安全性和实用性。

<h3 id="244">[244] Towards In-Depth Root Cause Localization for Microservices with Multi-Agent Recursion-of-Thought</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, Fault Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 本文提出RCLAgent框架，用于微服务系统的根因定位，通过多智能体递归思维和并行推理机制，沿追踪图分解诊断过程，在多个公开基准上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="245">[245] REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peicheng Wu, Zhenyu Bu, Runze Ma, Lin Du
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, Knowledge Distillation, Neural Decoding
- **Summary**: 本文提出REALM框架，通过回顾性蒸馏方法实现因果性局部场电位解码，将预训练的双向教师模型知识迁移到实时部署的因果学生模型，在参数减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍的同时提升了解码性能。

<h3 id="246">[246] Unlocking Complex Visual Generation via Closed-Loop Verified Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14876) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出闭环视觉推理（CLVR）框架，将视觉语言逻辑规划与像素级扩散生成深度耦合，引入步骤级视觉验证的数据引擎和代理提示强化学习，在复杂视觉生成任务上接近商业闭源模型性能。

<h3 id="247">[247] SurgicalMamba: Dual-Path SSD with State Regramming for Online Surgical Phase Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14889) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Surgical Video Analysis, State Space Model, Action Recognition

- **Summary**: 本文提出SurgicalMamba模型，基于Mamba2的结构化状态空间对偶性构建，引入双路径SSD块、强度调制步进和状态重编程三个组件，在七个公开手术阶段识别基准上达到最优性能。

<h3 id="248">[248] Your CLIP has 164 dimensions of noise: Exploring the embeddings covariance eigenspectrum of contrastively pretrained vision-language transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14893) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakub Grzywaczewski, Dawid Płudowski, Przemysław Biecek
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Representation Learning, Dimensionality Reduction
- **Summary**: 本文通过协方差矩阵谱分解分析对比预训练视觉语言模型的嵌入空间，发现潜在空间中存在大量共享噪声维度，剪枝这些噪声维度对下游任务性能无害甚至有益。

<h3 id="249">[249] Critic-Driven Voronoi-Quantization for Distilling Deep RL Policies to Explainable Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14897) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Senne Deproost, Denis Steckelmacher, Ann Nowé
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Interpretability
- **Venue**: AAMAS 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种名为Critic-Driven Voronoi State Partitioning的方法，通过利用原始策略的价值网络将黑盒控制策略划分为多个区域，并在每个区域优化简单的线性模型，从而在保持性能的同时实现策略的可解释性。

<h3 id="250">[250] Slot-MPC: Goal-Conditioned Model Predictive Control with Object-Centric Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14937) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, Object-Centric Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Slot-MPC框架，通过学习以物体为中心的表示来构建世界模型，并利用基于梯度的模型预测控制实现动作规划，使智能体能够适应未见过的场景。

<h3 id="251">[251] Not All Symbols Are Equal: Importance-Aware Constellation Design for Semantic Communication</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14940) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Albert Shaju, Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas, Mayukh Roy Chowdhury
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Semantic Communication, Wireless Networks, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种联合语义-物理层框架，通过语义关键性指示器对概念进行任务相关性评分，并设计语义感知的M-QAM星座图，使任务关键符号获得更好的保护。

<h3 id="252">[252] MHSA: A Lightweight Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention in LVLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14966) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei Ding, Yilin Li, Yudong Zhang, Ruobing Xie, Xingwu Sun, Jiansheng Chen, Yu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Vision-Language Model, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文提出MHSA框架，通过学习修正跨模态注意力模式来缓解大型视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题，使用三层MLP生成器在推理时替换原始注意力，无需修改模型参数。

<h3 id="253">[253] Viverra: Text-to-Code with Guarantees</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14972) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoze Wu, Rocky Klopfenstein, Keith Farkas, Nina Narodytska
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Formal Methods, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 本文提出Viverra系统，在生成代码的同时自动生成形式化验证的断言，通过有界模型检查器验证安全性和正确性属性，帮助用户理解生成的程序。

<h3 id="254">[254] MicroscopyMatching: Towards a Ready-to-use Framework for Microscopy Image Analysis in Diverse Conditions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaofei Hui, Haoxuan Qu, Hossein Rahmani, Shuohong Wang, Jeff W. Lichtman, Jun Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Object Tracking
- **Summary**: 本文提出MicroscopyMatching框架，将多种显微镜图像分析任务（分割、跟踪、计数）统一为匹配问题，利用预训练潜在扩散模型的匹配能力，实现跨多样化设置的就绪可用分析。

<h3 id="255">[255] Second-Order Actor-Critic Methods for Discounted MDPs via Policy Hessian Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14982](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14982) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization, Actor-Critic
- **Summary**: 本文针对折扣奖励强化学习场景，提出二阶Actor-Critic方法，利用Hessian-向量积计算实现曲率感知的参数更新，在双时间尺度框架下保证稳定性。

<h3 id="256">[256] Agreement, Diversity, and Polarization Indices for Approval Elections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14983](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14983) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Piotr Faliszewski, Jitka Mertlová, Krzysztof Sornat, Stanisław Szufa, Tomasz Wąs
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Computational Social Choice, Social Network Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出用于批准选举的一致性、多样性和极化指标，这些指标相对于饱和度进行归一化，并使用它们绘制了批准选举的新地图，分析了来自多个数据源的真实选举数据。

<h3 id="257">[257] Sat3DGen: Comprehensive Street-Level 3D Scene Generation from Single Satellite Image</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14984) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ming Qian, Zimin Xia, Changkun Liu, Shuailei Ma, Wen Wang, Zeran Ke, Bin Tan, Hang Zhang, Gui-Song Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Scene Generation, Image Generation
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/qianmingduowan/Sat3DGen)
- **Summary**: 本文提出Sat3DGen，一种从单张卫星图像生成街道级3D场景的方法，采用几何优先策略解决卫星到街景视角差距大和监督稀疏的问题。该方法通过引入新颖的几何约束和透视视角训练策略，在几何精度和真实感方面取得了显著提升。

<h3 id="258">[258] Predicting Response to Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy in Ovarian Cancer from CT Baseline Using Multi-Loss Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14991) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francesco Pastori, Francesca Fati, Marina Rosanu, Luigi De Vitis, Lucia Ribero, Gabriella Schivardi, Giovanni Damiano Aletti, Nicoletta Colombo, Jvan Casarin, Francesco Multinu, Elena De Momi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Cancer Prognosis
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种深度学习框架，利用预处理CT图像预测卵巢癌患者对新辅助化疗的反应。该方法通过自动提取的3D病灶掩码、预训练图像编码器和注意力聚合模块，结合对比学习和困难负样本挖掘，在280名患者的队列上实现了0.73的ROC-AUC。

<h3 id="259">[259] Towards Gaze-Informed AI Disclosure Interfaces: Eye-Tracking Attentional and Cognitive Load While Reading AI-Assisted News</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pooja Prajod, Hannes Cools, Thomas Röggla, Pablo Cesar, Abdallah El Ali
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, AI Transparency, Eye Tracking
- **Summary**: 本研究通过眼动追踪和NASA-TLX测量，探讨了AI使用披露对新闻读者注意力和认知负荷的影响。研究发现简短披露会导致更高的注视时长和眼跳次数，而详细披露不会增加额外负担，为设计自适应披露界面提供了依据。

<h3 id="260">[260] Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rebecca Handler, Suhana Bedi, Nigam Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Medical AI, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文定义并评估了大语言模型中的"过早下结论"问题，即在不确定情况下提供答案而非选择澄清或拒绝。实验表明前沿LLM在医学任务中即使正确答案被移除仍有55-82%的错误行动率，安全导向提示可降低但无法消除此问题。

<h3 id="261">[261] Boosting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards via Randomly Selected Few-Shot Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15012) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Yan, Alexander G. Schwing, Yu-Xiong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出FEST，一种基于少量样本演示引导的强化学习方法，用于提升可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)在困难问题上的样本效率。该方法仅使用128个随机选择的演示样本，通过监督信号、策略信号和衰减权重三个关键组件，在数学和编程基准上取得了优异表现。

<h3 id="262">[262] COTCAgent: Preventive Consultation via Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15016) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihan Deng, Xiaozhen Zhong, Chuanzhi Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Reasoning, Clinical Decision Support
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FrankDengAI/COTCAgent/)
- **Summary**: 本文提出COTCAgent，一个用于纵向电子健康记录推理的分层框架，解决LLM在统计推理和长程时序依赖方面的不足。该框架包含时序统计适配器、思维链补全层和有界补全模块，在自建数据集和HealthBench上分别达到90.47%和70.41%的准确率。

<h3 id="263">[263] Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kiljae Lee, Ziqi Liu, Weijing Tang, Yuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Valuation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了广义优先级感知Shapley值(GPASV)，定义在任意有向加权优先级图上，解决了现有方法要求成对优先级必须无环的限制，并将其应用于LLM集成评估。

<h3 id="264">[264] SemaTune: Semantic-Aware Online OS Tuning with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15026) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Georgios Liargkovas, Mihir Nitin Joshi, Hubertus Franke, Kostis Kaffes
- **Subjects**: cs.OS; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Data Valuation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出广义优先感知Shapley值(GPASV)，将优先感知估值扩展到任意有向加权优先图，突破了现有方法对二元和无环优先关系的限制。该方法应用于Chatbot Arena偏好图上的LLM集成估值，展示了不同优先平衡如何产生不同的估值结果。

<h3 id="265">[265] WARD: Adversarially Robust Defense of Web Agents Against Prompt Injections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15030) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tri Cao, Yulin Chen, Hieu Cao, Yibo Li, Khoi Le, Thong Nguyen, Yuexin Li, Yufei He, Yue Liu, Shuicheng Yan, Bryan Hooi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Prompt Injection, Adversarial Robustness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/caothientri2001vn/WARD-WebAgent)
- **Summary**: 本文提出WARD，一个用于保护Web代理免受提示注入攻击的防御模型，包含大规模数据集WARD-Base和WARD-PIG，以及自适应对抗攻击训练框架A3T。该方法在分布外基准上实现近乎完美的召回率，同时保持低误报率和不增加延迟。

<h3 id="266">[266] AI Knows When It's Being Watched: Functional Strategic Action and Contextual Register Modulation in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15034) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vinicius Covas, Jorge Alberto Hidalgo Toledo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Optimization, LLM Application, System Design
- **Summary**: 本文提出SemaTune，一个基于LLM的在线操作系统调优框架，通过语义理解旋钮模式和遥测数据来优化系统参数。该方法在13个工作负载上相比默认设置提升72.5%，同时通过类型化验证避免进入性能降级区域。

<h3 id="267">[267] EverAnimate: Minute-Scale Human Animation via Latent Flow Restoration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wuyang Li, Yang Gao, Mariam Hassan, Lan Feng, Wentao Pan, Po-Chien Luan, Alexandre Alahi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Evaluation, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本研究探讨了LLM多智能体系统是否会对感知到的社会观察情境产生系统性语言适应。实验表明被监控条件产生更高的TTR变化，且人类评估比自动化AI监控引发更强的语域规范化，对AI治理和审计具有启示意义。

<h3 id="268">[268] SpeakerLLM: A Speaker-Specialized Audio-LLM for Speaker Understanding and Verification Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15044](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15044) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: KiHyun Nam, Jungwoo Heo, Siu Bae, Ha-Jin Yu, Joon Son Chung
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Speaker Verification, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文提出SpeakerLLM，一个专门用于说话人理解和验证推理的音频LLM框架，统一了说话人画像、录音条件理解、说话人比较和验证推理。该方法使用分层说话人分词器捕获多粒度说话人证据，提升了说话人理解和验证能力。

<h3 id="269">[269] TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15053](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15053) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anurup Ganguli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, LLM Training, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出TFGN，一种用于LLM持续预训练的架构覆盖层，无需回放或任务标签即可避免灾难性遗忘。该方法在LLaMA 3.1 8B规模上实现-0.007的后向迁移和99.59%的域间梯度分离，并包含元控制和算子级规划的扩展。

<h3 id="270">[270] NeuroTrain: Surveying Local Learning Rules for Spiking Neural Networks with an Open Benchmarking Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15058) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alessio Caviglia, Filippo Marostica, Roberta Bardini, Alessandro Savino, Stefano Di Carlo
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Neuromorphic Computing, Survey
- **Summary**: 本综述提供了脉冲神经网络训练算法的全面分类体系，涵盖代理梯度反向传播、局部学习规则、生物启发可塑性机制、ANN到SNN转换等方法。同时发布NeuroTrain开源基准框架，支持跨数据集、架构和训练机制的一致评估。

<h3 id="271">[271] On the Cultural Anachronism and Temporal Reasoning in Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15071) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mukul Ranjan, Prince Jha, Khushboo Kumari, Zhiqiang Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark, Temporal Reasoning, Cultural Heritage
- **Summary**: 该论文识别了视觉语言模型在解释历史文物时的文化时代错位问题，并引入TAB-VLM基准数据集来评估模型在印度文化遗产上的时间推理能力，揭示了现有模型的显著不足。

<h3 id="272">[272] Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15077) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guangyu Feng, Huanzhi Mao, Prabal Dutta, Joseph E. Gonzalez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出AsyncFC，一个纯执行层框架，无需模型微调或协议修改即可实现LLM异步函数调用。该方法允许模型解码与函数执行重叠以及函数间并行，显著降低端到端任务完成时间同时保持任务准确率。

<h3 id="273">[273] ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyin Zhang, Zihan Liao, Hang Yu, Peng Di, Rui Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text Embedding, Multilingual Learning, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ML-Embed，一套基于三维Matryoshka学习框架的多语言文本嵌入模型，旨在解决计算成本高、语言覆盖窄和缺乏透明度三大障碍。该模型在MTEB基准测试中创下新纪录，尤其在低资源语言上表现优异，并开源了所有模型、数据和代码。

<h3 id="274">[274] Novel Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam Optimiser for Vehicular Accident Injury Severity Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15083](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15083) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel Asare Kyei, Alimatu Saadia-Yussiff, Maame G. Asante-Mensah, Abdul Lateef-Yussiff, Charles Roland Haruna, Derry Emmanuel
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Time Series Classification
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DBS-Adam优化器，通过基于梯度范数和批次损失的难度分数动态调整学习率，有效解决了不平衡序列数据上的训练问题。该方法在车辆事故伤害严重程度预测任务上达到95.22%的准确率，显著优于现有优化器。

<h3 id="275">[275] Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Renning Pang, Tian Lan, Leyuan Liu, Xiaoming Huang, Piao Tong, Xiaosong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Long Context, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Self-Recall Thinking (SRT)框架，通过识别有用的历史对话轮次并选择性回忆和推理，解决多轮对话中的长程依赖问题。该方法在多个数据集上实现了4.7%的F1提升和14.7%的延迟降低。

<h3 id="276">[276] Logging Policy Design for Off-Policy Evaluation</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了CausalReasoningBenchmark，包含173个查询和132个真实世界数据集，用于分别评估因果推理中的识别（研究设计）和估计（数值执行）两个步骤。实验表明LLM在识别策略的高层选择上正确率达79%，但完整识别规范正确率仅34%，瓶颈在于研究设计的细节而非计算。

<h3 id="307">[307] A Minimal Agent for Automated Theorem Proving</h3>

<h3 id="312">[312] Catching the Infection Before It Spreads: Foresight-Guided Defense in Multi-Agent Systems</h3>

<h3 id="315">[315] The Compliance Trap: How Structural Constraints Degrade Frontier AI Metacognition Under Adversarial Pressure</h3>

<h3 id="317">[317] Attractor Geometry of Transformer Memory: From Conflict Arbitration to Confident Hallucination</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05686) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiyao Liang, Risto Miikkulainen, Ila Fiete
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Interpretability, Memory Architecture

<h3 id="364">[364] Achieving Approximate Symmetry Is Exponentially Easier than Exact Symmetry</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了L2R框架，用于改进混合专家模型(MoE)中的路由机制。该方法通过低秩潜在路由空间和饱和内积评分来控制路由函数的Lipschitz行为，从而改善路由几何和专家区分度，在语言和视觉MoE模型上均取得了性能提升。

<h3 id="370">[370] Neural Signals Generate Clinical Notes in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22197) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jathurshan Pradeepkumar, Zheng Chen, Jimeng Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Report Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Jathurshan0330/CELM)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CELM，首个能够从长时程EEG记录生成临床报告的临床EEG-语言基础模型。该模型整合了预训练EEG基础模型和语言模型，在包含约11,000小时EEG记录的大规模数据集上进行训练，临床专家评估显示其生成的报告更具临床连贯性和诊断可靠性。

<h3 id="371">[371] PaAno: Patch-Based Representation Learning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了PaAno，一种轻量级的时间序列异常检测方法，通过基于补丁的表示学习实现快速高效的检测。该方法使用1D卷积神经网络和三元组损失训练，在TSB-AD基准测试中取得了最先进性能，显著优于包括大型架构在内的现有方法。

<h3 id="372">[372] Boosting LLM Reasoning via Human-Inspired Reward Shaping</h3>

<h3 id="373">[373] GhostCite: A Large-Scale Analysis of Citation Validity in the Age of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06718) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zuyao Xu, Yuqi Qiu, Lu Sun, Fasheng Miao, Fubin Wu, Xiang Li, Xinyi Wang, Haozhe Lu, Zhengze Zhang, Yuxin Hu, Jialu Li, Luo Jin, Feng Zhang, Rui Luo, Xinran Liu, Yingxian Li, Jiaji Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Citation Analysis, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文开发了citeb框架用于大规模引用验证，并系统研究了LLM时代的引用有效性问题。研究发现LLM在引用生成任务中的幻觉率高达14.23%-94.93%，2025年AI/ML和安全领域论文中的无效引用增加了80.9%，揭示了幽灵引用对学术诚信的系统性威胁。

<h3 id="374">[374] VLRS-Bench: A Vision-Language Reasoning Benchmark for Remote Sensing</h3>

<h3 id="375">[375] Proximal Action Replacement for Behavior Cloning Actor-Critic in Offline Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="376">[376] Krause Synchronization Transformers</h3>

<h3 id="377">[377] ArGEnT: Arbitrary Geometry-encoded Transformer for Operator Learning</h3>

<h3 id="378">[378] Finding Interpretable Prompt-Specific Circuits in Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="379">[379] Numerical exploration of the range of shape functionals using neural networks</h3>

<h3 id="380">[380] Artificial Intelligence Specialization in the European Union: Underexplored Role of the Periphery at NUTS-3 Level</h3>

- **Authors**: Victor Herrero-Solana, Carmen Gálvez
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Citation Analysis, AI Policy, Research Methodology
- **Summary**: 本研究分析了2015-2024年欧洲NUTS-3区域的AI研究分布情况。研究发现，虽然巴黎、华沙和马德里等大都市在绝对发表量上占主导，但最高的相对AI专业化水平集中在边缘地区，特别是东欧和西班牙，揭示了区域专业化与引用影响之间的弱相关性。

<h3 id="382">[382] CUICurate: A GraphRAG-based Framework for Automated Clinical Concept Curation for NLP applications</h3>

<h3 id="383">[383] Grokking Finite-Dimensional Algebra</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.RA
- **Tags**: Grokking, Deep Learning Theory, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了在有限维代数乘法学习中的grokking现象，将先前关于群运算的研究扩展到非结合、非交换和非单位元代数。研究揭示了代数性质（如交换性、结合性）如何影响grokking的出现和时机，以及结构张量的性质如何影响泛化，为神经网络的泛化动力学提供了新见解。

<h3 id="384">[384] MPU: Towards Secure and Privacy-Preserving Knowledge Unlearning for Large Language Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种LLM的快慢学习框架，将模型参数作为慢权重，优化后的上下文作为快权重，在推理任务上比纯RL训练样本效率提升3倍，同时减少了灾难性遗忘并保持了更好的可塑性。

<h3 id="434">[434] WriteSAE: Sparse Autoencoders for Recurrent State</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12770) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jack Young
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Interpretability, State Space Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/JackYoung27/writesae)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了WriteSAE，首个能够分解和编辑状态空间模型及混合循环语言模型矩阵缓存写入的稀疏自编码器，通过将解码器原子分解为原生写入形状，实现了在循环写入位置的有效行为干预。

<h3 id="435">[435] Context Training with Active Information Seeking</h3>

<h3 id="436">[436] Scaling few-shot spoken word classification with generative meta-continual learning</h3>

- **Authors**: Louise Beyers, Batsirayi Mupamhi Ziki, Ruan van der Merwe
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Speech Processing, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 本文研究将少样本口语词分类扩展到1000个类别的潜力，每个类别仅提供5个样本。作者采用生成式元持续学习(GeMCL)算法训练模型，发现该方法能产生稳定的性能表现，与冻结HuBERT加分类器头的方法性能相当，同时适应速度快2000倍，训练时间减少两个数量级。

<h3 id="441">[441] Inducing Overthink: Hierarchical Genetic Algorithm-based DoS Attack on Black-Box Large Language Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13338](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13338) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuqiang Wang, Wei Cao, Jiaqi Weng, Jialing Tao, Licheng Pan, Hui Xue, Zhixuan Chu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EndlessCao/Overthink-HGA)
- **Summary**: 本文研究大型推理模型(LRMs)在面对不完整或逻辑不一致输入时产生的"过度思考"现象，揭示其可作为拒绝服务攻击的资源耗尽向量。作者提出一种基于层次遗传算法的黑盒框架，通过系统性地扰动输入问题的逻辑结构来诱导过度思考，在MATH基准上实现最高26.1倍的输出长度增加，并展示了强跨模型迁移性。
